Works standalone — does not require prior evaluation/inspection skills, but will use their outputs if available in agent_outputs/. Do not use for non-BioThings data pipelines or general ETL. Use whenever the user works in a project with a `.biotope/` directory or a `graph/` workspace… ([biocypher/biotope](https://github.com/biocypher/biotope/tree/HEAD/skills/biotope-croissant) · ★6)

- **biotransformation-enumeration**: Use when you have a known parent drug chemical formula and aim to predict its metabolite landscape prior to or during high-resolution mass spectrometry analysis (mzML). - **biotransformation-prediction-across-microbiota-contexts**: Use when you have one or more small-molecule chemical structures (as SMILES, MOL, or SDF) and need to systematically explore their fate across mammalian biotransformation, human gut microbial degradation, or environmental (soil/aquatic) microbial degradation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/biotransformation-prediction-across-microbiota-contexts) · 2 copies)
- **biotransformation-reaction-rule-matching**: Use when untargeted LC-HRMS feature tables lack library MS2 matches but samples are enzymatic incubations (e.g., human liver S9) of known parent xenobiotics, so candidates can be derived in silico from reaction rules rather than reference spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/biotransformation-reaction-rule-matching))
- **biotransformation-rule-application**: Use when an MS/MS query spectrum is unannotated but a spectral-similarity neighbor (anchor) with a known SMILES and a precursor mass difference is available, and candidate molecular structures for the suspect are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/biotransformation-rule-application))
- **biotransformation-rule-application**: Use when when you have a small-molecule structure (SMILES, MOL, or SDF format) and need to predict its fate in soil or aquatic environments through microbial degradation pathways. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/biotransformation-rule-application))
- **biotransformation-rule-application-to-metabolites**: Use when you have untargeted metabolomics data with unknown or ambiguous molecular identities, anchor metabolites (known structures in SMILES or MOL format), and a curated database of biotransformation rules (e.g., from KEGG, RetroRules, or domain-specific repositories). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/biotransformation-rule-application-to-metabolites) · 2 copies)
- **biotransformation-rule-encoding**: Use when you have untargeted metabolomics data with unknown metabolite structures and need to generate plausible candidate products by systematically applying known enzymatic or chemical transformation rules. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/biotransformation-rule-encoding) · 2 copies)
- **biotransformation-rule-extraction-from-database**: Use when you need to populate or reconstruct a biotransformation prediction module and have access to a curated biotransformation database (such as EAWAG BBD) that documents empirically validated microbial degradation pathways, enzyme-substrate relationships, and reaction outcomes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/biotransformation-rule-extraction-from-database))
- **biotransformation-rule-mass-difference-matching**: Use when a query spectrum has high spectral similarity to a known anchor molecule in a molecular network, and the precursor mass difference between suspect and anchor must be converted into a list of plausible biotransformation-derived candidate structures. - **biotransformation-rule-normalization**: Use when you need to convert a raw reaction dataset (e.g. RetroRules dump, KEGG reaction list) into a clean set of reaction-center substructure rules that can be applied to query molecules by PROXIMAL2. - **biotransformer-batch-metabolite-prediction**: Use when a compiled chemical list (SMILES/structures, e.g. the &gt;20,000-chemical human exposome set) needs in-silico metabolite prediction across EC-based, CYP450, phase II, and gut microbial biotransformation modules. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/biotransformer-batch-metabolite-prediction))
- **biotransformer-one-step-transformation-recapitulation**: Use when you have a set of generated SMILES and a set of known metabolite SMILES and need to verify that the generated structures resemble plausible metabolic transformation products rather than arbitrary synthetic compounds. - **biotransformer-prediction-curation**: Use when candidate drug metabolites detected by untargeted LC-HRMS need to be annotated as known/predicted or novel by matching detected precursor masses against a BioTransformer 3.0 prediction database. - **biotransformer-prediction-matching**: Use when curating LC-HRMS-detected postincubation (24-hour) drug metabolite features from human liver S9 incubations against BioTransformer 3.0 predicted metabolites to label each as predicted or unpredicted (novel). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/biotransformer-prediction-matching))
- **biotransformer-reaction-type-curation**: Use when S9-incubation LC-HRMS annotation tables carry BioTransformer 3.0 reaction-type tags and you need per-reaction detected/predicted counts (oxidation, glucuronidation, etc.). - **biotransformer-tp-structure-prediction**: Use when confirmed parent suspects (e.g., carbamazepine, benzotriazole) from LC-HRMS suspect screening need candidate transformation product structures before TP suspect screening and componentization.
